## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in one of your repos fixed while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 여러분의 일을 같이 해드리는 파트너예요. 예를 들어, 깨진 테스트나 막힌 빌드가 있는 저장소를 가리키시면 제가 원인을 찾아 고쳐볼게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 편하게 쉬어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証せずに速さと軽さを優先します。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Rebase reescribe tus commits para que queden encima de la rama base, dejando un historial lineal, ideal para ramas personales o de feature que aún no has compartido. Merge conserva el historial real y es lo más seguro cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otras personas, porque no reescribe commits que otros puedan tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序送达、丢了会重传；UDP 不保证，只管尽快发出去，换来更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to tidy it up before review, so it lands cleanly on the latest base. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have pulled.
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我把登录页重做拆成三件，按顺序一件件做，每件做完告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页重做 1/3：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本件（第 1 件）：把登录页的登录表单换成新的输入框组件。只做这一件，不要接短信验证码，也不要写端到端测试（那是第 2、3 件）。 ⏎  ⏎ 已定的决定：按顺序做，本件是三件里的第一件。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单里的输入框都换成新的输入框组件，原有的字段、校验和提交行为保持不变。 ⏎ - 项目里已有的测试和类型检查/构建能通过，并把运行结果（命令和通过情况）写进报告。 ⏎ - 报告里列出改动的文件和新组件的位置。 ⏎  ⏎ 不在范围内：不推送、不合并到主分支、不开 PR。改动放在本任务自己的分支上提交即可。不要碰短信验证码和端到端测试相关的代码。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/ducter 仓库。 ⏎  ⏎ The things asked (the user sees each under their message, each ticking off on its own): ⏎ 1. 登录表单换成新的输入框组件 ⏎ In report_to_main, give each one's state in `asks`, in this order, with a short note of what came of it (the answer, "added", "PR opened"). A progress report with `asks` ticks off the ones already done while you go on.
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 2/3：接上短信验证码 | group: 登录页重做
  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页重做 3/3：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配所有符合的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it's about to make, before touching the actual data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to finish or undo the interrupted changes, so committed data survives and nothing is left half-written.
```

### board-offer-go-zh

```
(earlier) user: 每天早上帮我看一下周五北京到上海的高铁票价，600 以下的告诉我。
(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，看板里已经填上周五的票价：G1 和 G3 都是 553 元，600 以下有 2 班。每天早上的新数据会自动更新进去。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1954 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-card">
    <div class="k-sum">
      <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京 → 上海 · 600 元以下</div>
      <div class="k-sum-value" id="low"></div>
      <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">最近一次看到的班次</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <p class="k-from" id="from"></p>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return '¥' + n; }
function render(d){
  d = d || {};
  var trains = d.trains || [];
  var limit = d.limit || 600;
  var under = trains.filter(function(t){ return t.price < limit; });
  var low = document.getElementById('low');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var from = document.getElementById('from');
  if (!trains.length) {
    low.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    sub.textContent = '每天早上看一次，600 以下的班次会记在这里';
    list.innerHTML = '';
    from.textContent = '';
    return;
  }
  var min = Math.min.apply(null, trains.map(function(t){ return t.price; }));
  low.textContent = fmt(min) + ' 起';
  sub.textContent = '600 元以下 ' + under.length + ' 班';
  list.innerHTML = '';
  trains.forEach(function(t){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = '二等座' + (t.left != null ? '余 ' + t.left + ' 张' : '');
    main.appendChild(s);
    var v = document.createElement('div');
    v.className = 'k-row-value';
    v.textContent = fmt(t.price) + (t.price < limit ? '' : ' · 超 600');
    v.style.color = t.price < limit ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(v);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  from.textContent = (d.checked ? d.checked + ' 看的' : '');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render(ducter.get('fares'));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":{"day":"周五","route":"北京 → 上海","limit":600,"checked":"第 3 天","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"left":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"left":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"left":null}]}}

.k-app{font-family:-apple-system,system-ui,sans-serif;color:var(--d-ink);background:var(--d-bg);padding:16px;max-width:360px;margin:0 auto}
.k-sum{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:14px;margin-bottom:16px}

function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
function render(){
  var nights = ducter.get('nights') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
  if(!nights.length){ list.innerHTML=''; empty.style.display='block'; return; }
  empty.style.display='none';
  var last = nights[0];
  latest.textContent = 'Night ' + last.night + ': ' + last.writes + ' writes/s';
  list.innerHTML = nights.map(function(n){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div><div class="k-row-main">Night ' + esc(n.night) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + esc(n.p99) + ' · stalls ' + esc(n.stalls == null ? '—' : n.stalls) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + esc(n.writes) + ' /s</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":3,"writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0},{"night":2,"writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":null}]}

<script>
(function () {
  var S = { days: 1, items: [] };
  var cur = 1;
  var editing = null;
  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElemen … rsist();
    clearForm();
    render();
  });

  $('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!(amt > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    var what = $('what').value.trim() || '未填';
    var amount = Math.round(amt * 100) / 100;
    if (editing !== null) {
      S.items = S.items.map(function (x) {
        return x.id === editing ? { id: x.id, day: x.day, what: what, amount: amount } : x;
      });
    } else {
      S.items.push({ id: Date.now(), day: cur, what: what, amount: amount });
    }
    persist();
    clearForm();
    $('addBox').open = false;
    render();
  });

  $('del').addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (editing === null) return;
    S.items = S.items.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== editing; });
    persist();
    clearForm();
    $('addBox').open = false;
    render();
  });

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trip')).then(function (v) {
      if (v && typeof v === 'object' && Array.isArray(v.items)) {
        S = { days: v.days || 1, items: v.items };
      }
      if (cur > S.days) cur = S.days;
      render();
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const sum = xs.reduce((a, x) => a + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + sum;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden = xs.length > 0;
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
ducter.summary("3 笔,合计 ¥1966");
</script>

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done. It's a page you can keep, with the four PRs. Tap one when it lands and it gets struck through, and the count at the top drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2315 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still open</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tap a PR once it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<style>
  .k-row{width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:0;color:inherit;font:inherit;cursor:pointer;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:10px 0}
  .k-box{flex:0 0 20px;height:20px;border:2px solid var(--d-line,#999);border-radius:5px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-top:2px;font-size:13px;color:var(--d-good,#2a7)}
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-box{border-color:var(--d-good,#2a7)}
  .k-row[aria-pressed="true"] .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;opacity:.5}
  .k-row-main{flex:1}
  .k-row-title{font-weight:600}
  .k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted,#888);font-size:13px;margin-top:2px}
</style>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var done = {};
  function esc(x){ return String(x).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c){ return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]; }); }
  function paint(){
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var open = 0;
    list.innerHTML = '';
    PRS.forEach(function(p){
      var on = !!done[p.id];
      if (!on) open++;
      var b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'k-row';
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false');
      b.innerHTML = '<span class="k-box">' + (on ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="k-row-main"><div class="k-row-title">' + esc(p.t) + ' · ' + esc(p.d) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(p.s) + '</div></span>';
      b.onclick = function(){
        done[p.id] = !done[p.id];
        ducter.set('done', done);
        paint();
      };
      list.appendChild(b);
    });
    document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tap a PR once it lands';
    ducter.summary(open + ' open');
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function(v){ done = v || {}; paint(); });
  ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function(v){ done = v || {}; paint(); }); });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter412","t":"wey-gu/ducter #412","d":"Compaction cut","s":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem10287","t":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","d":"Retry on 502","s":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb1592","t":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","d":"Flaky compaction test","s":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter418","t":"wey-gu/ducter #418","d":"Apps bar","s":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个 3D 风格的米黄色笔记本电脑插图：深蓝色边框，屏幕是空白的，键盘和触控板都画得很清楚，整体柔和、圆润。

适合放在空状态里。它温和不吓人，屏幕空着正好对应"还没有内容"的意思。不过屏幕上没有任何东西，单独放可能让人看不出是什么状态，最好配一行文字，比如"还没有文档"或"这里暂时没有内容"，再给一个操作按钮。如果你的空状态跟电脑、文件或工作内容有关，这张图很合适；如果是别的主题，比如收藏、消息，可能就不太贴切了。

另外，这张图只有 320×246，放到页面上时建议缩到 160 到 200 像素宽。如果能拿到透明背景的版本，深色模式下也会更好看。
```

(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 那个任务里补一条测试，覆盖正好到期的那一刻。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户的原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的 TTL 测试基础上，再加一条测试：用同一个假时钟，把时间推到恰好等于过期时刻，断言这一刻的值已经视为过期（或者按现有语义的边界处理，并在测试名里写清楚是哪一种）。再测一下刚好早 1 毫秒时仍然有效。

完成标准：新测试通过，整套 TTL 测试连跑 20 次都通过；报告里附上测试输出。
不要做的事：不要改成真实计时；不要合并、推送或开 PR；不要动 values() 那个任务的改动。
